<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, January 13, 2015 
PRESENT: Jesse Thompson, Chairman; David Tenney, Supervisor; Jason Whiting, Supervisor; and Dawnafe 
Whitesinger, Supervisor were all present. Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor wa s not present. Supervisor Nez was  
excused to attend the Navajo Nation Inauguration.  
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Chairman Thompson.  
Happy Birthday was sung to Supervisor Tenney and Supervisor Whiting. 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Election of Chairman and Vice -Chairman for 2015 : Supervisor Whiting  made a motion  to elect 
Supervisor Tenney  as Chairman and Supervisor Whitesinger  as Vice -Chairman for 2015; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Presentation of a Plaque of Appreciation for 2014 Service to Chairman Jesse Thompson : Chairman 
Tenney thanked Supervisor Thompson for his service as Chairman during the 2014 year.  Chairman 
Tenney presented Supervisor Thompson  with an engraved clock. Appreciation  was expressed to 
Supervisor Thompson from the Board for his leadership as Chairman.  
 
At 9:20 a.m. Supervisor Thompson was excused to attend the Navajo Nation Inauguration.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC : No public comments.  Chairman Tenney explained that item number 12 will be 
moved up on the agenda, for the public to speak regarding that item.  
CONSENT AGENDA : a) Minutes: November 18, 2014 Special Board Meeting; November 25, 2014; and 
December 9, 2014 b) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 December 2014 c) Clerk of Superior Court Report 
November & December 2014 d) Juvenile Probation Report November & December 2014 e) Justice Court Reports: 
Holbrook Precinct #1, November & December; Winslow Precinct #2, October & December; Snowflake Precinct 
#3, November & December; Kayenta Precinct #4, November; Show Low Precinct #5, November; Pinetop -
Lakeside Precinct #6, November & December 2014 f) Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, September & 
October/November; Winslow Precinct #2, October, November & December; Snowflake Precinct #3, November; 
Show Low Precinct #5, November & December; Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, November & December 2014 g) 
Approve Arizona Attorney General’s Office to represent Navajo County in SolarCity Corporation v. ADOR et. 
al. in connection with the 2015 complaint (TX2014 -000189) h) Approve Arizona Attorney General’s Office to 
represent Navajo County in Novo Biopower, LLC v ADOR and Navajo County (TX-2014000244) in connection 
with the 2015 complaint i) Adoption of Resolution Number 01 -15, cancelling the elections for the White 
Mountain Lake Special Road District and Timberland Acres Special Road Distric t; and appoint the candidate(s) 
or deeming the position vacant, pursuant to A.R.S. 48 -1404 (B) j) Approval of the 2015 Constable Salaries k) 
Grant Agreement #HT -14-2347 with City of Tucson for High Intensity Drug Trafficking Areas (HIDTA) 
Domestic Highway Enforcement (DHE) funding for $12,000 l) Cooperative Law Enforcement Agreement with 
USDA Forest Service contract #12 -LE-11030121-02 Operating and Financial Plan for $7,920 for law 
enforcement cooperative efforts for FY15 m) Approval to receive Right Of Way  from the Bureau of Land 
Management for Rock Art Road n) Thank you letter to Congresswoman Ann Kirkpatrick for ensuring that the 
President’s request for our project was included with the FY 15 Omnibus Bill o) Letter of Recommendation for 
Tom Osen: Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in the consent 
agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
EMPLOYEE RECOGNITION : Navajo County Recognizes  Michael Sandoval (Facilities Management), 
Anna Ericksen (Assessor’s Office), Ron Smith (Superior Court), and Leann Baker (Facilities Management) 
for their excellence in personal performance far exceeding organization expectations : Danielle Hancock 
recognized Michael Sandoval, Anna Ericksen, Ron Smith and Leann Baker for their excellence in personal 
performance. A short video was show of the nominees. Mrs. Hancock thanked each of the employees and honored 
them with a certificate of recognition, a $50.00 gift card and a leaf to put on the employee recognition tree.     
      PUSH PROGRAM: Presentation of the Navajo County PUSH (People United Supporting Health)  Program: 
Mercy Beach gave a brief presentation of the new Navajo County PUSH Program. Supervisor Whiting made 
comments thanking the committee for their work.  
      HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions: Eric Scott reviewed the 64 
item personnel action report. Supervisor Whiting made a motion  to approve the personnel action report as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
      NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT Board of Directors Session: CONSENT  
AGENDA: Supervisor Whitesinger made a motio n to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services 
District Board of Directors Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
a.  Amendment #1 of Contract #ADHS12 -021336 with the Arizona Department of Health Ser vices for   
Population Health Policy Initiative extended through June 30, 2015 in the amount of $59,390.00 
b.   Maternal, Infant and Early Childhood Home Visiting Contract with the Arizona Department of Health 
      Services effective January 1, 2015 in the amount of $102,000.00 
c. Teen Pregnancy Prevention Contract ADHS13-0344423 Amendment #2 with the Arizona Department of 
Health Services for 1/1/2015 to 12/31/2017 in the amount of $124,513.00 
Mary Herring, Health Director discussed the item . Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve the 
consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whiting . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
ADMINISTRATION/COUNTY ATTORNEY:  
a. Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 02 -15, approving and accepting the terms 
and conditions of the Real Estate Contract for the purchase of the Clay Springs School Property : Jason 
Moore addressed the Board regarding the item. Chairman Tenney thanked Jason for his work on this 
item. He further made comments r egarding the history of the purchase of the  Clay Springs School 
Property. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion  to adopt Resolution Number 02-15, approving and 
accepting the terms and conditions of the Real Estate Contract for the purchase of the Clay Springs School 
Property; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of a lease between Navajo County and the Clay Springs Heritage 
Foundation for the Clay Springs School Building property rec ently purchased by Navajo County : Jason 
Moore discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve the lease between Navajo 
County and the Clay Springs Heritage Foundation for the Clay Springs School Building property recently 
purchased by Navajo County; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.                   
c. Consideration and possible approval of the proposed Community Development Block Grant Projects for 
the FY 2015 Community Development Block Grant funding cycle. Approve project #1 for funding and if 
income survey does not validate low-mod income requirements approve project #’s 2, 3, & 4 as alternative

<!-- page 3 -->
projects to be funded: Lynda Young addressed the Board regarding this item. Mary French Jones made a 
clarification that the path is a 13foot pathway that was paved and graveled. Chairman Tenney opened 
the public hearing, there being no comments closed the public hearing.  Mr. Jayne made comments 
regarding the item. Lynda Young answered questions of the Board. Supervisor Whiting made a motion 
to approve the proposed Community Development Block Grant Projects for the FY 2015 Community 
Development Block Grant funding cycle. Approve project #1 for funding and if income survey does not 
validate low-mod income requirements approve project #’s 2, 3, & 4 as alternative projects to be funded; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of a liquor license transfer under license #06090035 for Silver Creek 
Golf Club located at 2051 Silver Lake Blvd, White Mountain Lakes, AZ : Sheila Malone discussed the 
item. Supervisor Whiting made a motion to approve a liquor license transfer under license #06090035 
for Silver Creek Golf  Club located at 2051 Silver Lake Blvd, White Mountain Lakes, AZ ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of all properties sold as auctioned on the back tax land on-line auction 
and approval to allow the Clerk of the Board to sell the unsold properties over the counter for the amount 
due: Melissa Buckley discussed the item.  Supervisor W hitesinger made a motion  to approve all 
properties sold as auctioned on the back tax land on -line auction and approval to allow the Clerk of the 
Board to sell the unsold properties over the counter for the amount due; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
ELECTIONS: Consideration and possible appointment of individuals to fill vacant Board of Directors positions 
on the Heber -Overgaard Sa nitary District Governing Board : Johnathan Roes , Election Director  discussed the 
item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to the appointment of individuals to fill vacant Board of Directors 
positions on the Heber-Overgaard Sanitary District Governing Board; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
      FINANCE: Consideration and possible approval of sale of surplus County and seized property by public online 
auction to be held in February 2015: Mary Springer discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion 
to approve of sale of su rplus County and seized property by public online auction to be held in February 2015; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting . Vote unanimous approving the motion. Mr. Jayne and Chairman 
Tenney thanked Mary Springer.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  
a. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 03-15, approving a 
Zone Change that will allow property owner Keith Moore to operate a Domestic Farm Winery on APN 
202-15-036C: Trent Larson addressed the Board regarding the zone change for APN 202 -15-036C and 
answered questions of the Board. Keith Moore, the applicant, addressed the Board requesting approval of 
the zone change. Supervisor Whiting  and Supervisor Whitesinger  made comments. Supervisor 
Whitesinger asked about the building on the site. M r. Larson answered. Chairman Tenney opened the 
public hearing. T here being no comments,  Chairman Tenney  closed the public hearing. Supervisor 
Whiting made a motion to adopt Resolution Number 03-15, approving a Zone Change that will allow 
property owner Keith Moore to operate a Domestic Farm Winery on APN 202-15-036C; motion seconded 
by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 04-15, approving a 
Special Use Permit to allow property owner Juan Rosalez -Perez to operate an auto body repair shop on

<!-- page 4 -->
APN 212-26-082L: Trent Larson discussed the item and answered questions of the Board.  Mr. Larson 
indicated that the Planning & Zoning Commission voted to deny the Spe cial Use Permit, however if the 
Board of Supervisors decides to approve this item, there are several conditions that would need to be put 
into place for this property. Mr. Larson outlined the conditions. Juan Rosalez -Perez, the applicant, 
addressed the Board requ esting approval. Chairman Tenney opened the public hearing. The following 
constituents addressed the Board in opposi tion of the Special Use Permit: Virginia  Pierre, Dale 
Kallenburger, John Cossey, Gary Muder, Mike Baldwin, Don Hansen,  Anthony Nelson, Patricia Moore, 
James Skousen, and Willie Waldron. Jose Rosalez addressed the Board in favor of the item. He indicated 
measures would be  taken to dispose of items to keep the environment safe . Grady Hillis addressed the 
Board in favor of the item. Chairman Tenney closed the public hearing, and expressed thanks to all those 
who addressed the Board.  Supervisor Whitesinger  thanked Mr. Pe rez for his ambition and 
entrepreneurship however, she indicated given all the information today , she made a motion to deny; 
Supervisor Whiting echoed the words of Supervisor Whitesinger. Chairman Tenney made comments.  
Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to deny the adoption of Resolution Number 04-15, approving 
a Special Use Permit to allow property owner Juan Rosalez-Perez to operate an auto body repair shop on 
APN 212-26-082; motion seconded by Supervisor Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE : Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Whiting reported on his ongoing discussions with partnerships on a local, state and federal level, 
regarding the Apache Railroad. He stated he met with NPC officials and a few local mayors regarding their goals 
and objectives. Supervisor Whiting indicated he attended Governor Doug Ducey’s Inauguration and the State 
of State address. Supervisor Whiting reported that he, Supervisor Tenney and Hunter Moore met with Navajo 
County allies at  the L egislature regarding the issues  that affect our county . He  expressed his appreciation to 
Senator Sylvia Allen for allowing him to sit  in the gallery at the inauguration and for her support of this area . 
Melissa Buckley,  the Cle rk of the Board was recognized b y Debra Chapman and Sheila Malone for her six 
continuous years of service to the Board of Sup ervisors. Chairman Tenney asked Mrs. Buckley to express her 
thoughts. She indicated it has been an honor to serve the Board, Mr. Jayne, the Elected Officials and the employees 
of Navajo County she works with. Chairman Tenney , Supervisor Whiting  and Supervisor Whitesinger  
expressed their appreciation for Mrs. Buckley’s  excellent service and support to the Board. Supervisor 
Whitesinger reported she attended the White Mountain Regional Transportation meeting with Bill Bess, Interim 
Public Works Director . Supervisor Whitesinger  stated herself, Hunter Moore and Cha irman Lupe taped an 
interview regarding her appointment as the  Sunrise Board of Director ’s P resident, with  the focus on the Ski 
Resort’s objectives. Supervisor Whitesinger indicated Chairman Lupe has  worked with the Apache Tribal 
Community for 50 years, and he is now  on in his 8th term as Chairman  of the White Mountain Apache Tribe . 
Supervisor Whitesinger  indicated the Navajo County Health Department hosted an Ebola exercise training 
which she and many community members attended. Brad Carlyon, County Attorney reported on his Washington 
D.C. trip for NACo’s State President’s and State Director’s meeting. He indicated he attended a legislative session 
while he was  there. Mr. Carlyon  commented on attending the opening session yesterday, and expressed his 
concerns about the State shifting the budget cuts onto the Counties. He indicated on Friday, Governor Ducey will 
introduce the proposed budget.  Chairman Tenney reported on the Governor’s Inauguration and the Opening 
Day Session of the Legislature. He reported on his trip to Washington D.C. with Supervisor Whiting in hopes 
of finding ways to keep the Apache Railroad here, and not lose that asset to our community. Chairman Tenney 
stated his concerns about the state budget’s shortfall and that since the County is a subsidiary of the State, counties 
sometimes pay the toll. He indicated he hopes to meet with the Senate and House leadership and the governor’s 
office to work through those things and help Navajo County come out on top. Chairman Tenney expressed his 
thanks to James Menlove, Finance Director and James Jayne and the team for making the counties budget work.

<!-- page 5 -->
ADJOURN: At 11:18 a.m. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whiting. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
__________________________________ 
 
David Tenney, Chairman  
 
ATTEST: 
 
 
 
 
 
DATE:  January 27, 2015 
__________________________________ 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
